# Competitive Analysis — Q3 2026

## Headline
We're leading our primary competitor, **Acme Corp**, on revenue, but they're closing the gap with a strong 12% growth rate.

## Revenue comparison

| Metric | Us (Q3, corrected) | Acme Corp (Q3) | Delta |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Revenue** | $4,150,000 | $3,800,000 | +$350,000 (ours) |
| **Growth rate** | — | 12% | — |
| **Market share** | 52.2% | 47.8% | +4.4 pts (ours) |

## Key observations

- **Absolute lead:** We're ahead by **$350K** in Q3 revenue. Note this uses the *corrected* figure ($4.15M) — the uncorrected $4.4M would have overstated our lead by $250K.
- **Relative lead:** Our advantage is **+9.2%** versus Acme (or −8.4% from Acme's perspective).
- **Market share:** We hold roughly **52%** of the combined revenue between the two of us.
- **Growth concern:** Acme's **12% growth rate** is a meaningful signal. If that pace continues, they'll narrow the gap quarter-over-quarter. At 12% growth, Acme's next Q3 would land around $4.25M — which would actually put them *ahead* of our current $4.15M. This is the one area where the competitive picture is dynamic, not static.

## Bottom line
We hold a solid current revenue lead, but the margin is narrower than the raw numbers suggest once growth is factored in. If this is a sustained competitive analysis, we should track Acme's growth trajectory closely — they're the threat to watch.
